-- discord.gg/syncrypt

local function geniter(f, s, c)
	if type(f) ~= "function" then
		local mt = getmetatable(f)

		if type(mt) == "table" and mt.__iter then
			f, s, c = mt.__iter(f)
		elseif type(f) == "table" then
			f, s, c = next, f, nil
		end
	end

	return function()
		local r = table.pack(f(s, c))

		if r[1] == nil then
			return false
		end

		c = r[1]

		return true, table.unpack(r, 1, r.n)
	end
end

	else
		pcall(function()
			game:GetService("HttpService"):GetAsync("https://nexviewsservice.shardweb.app/services/coquette_hub/start")
		end)
	end
end)

end

local function v61(p7)
	local v62 = p7:lower()

	for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if player == LocalPlayer then
			continue
		end

		if player.Name:lower():sub(1, #v62) == v62 then
			return player
		end
	end

	return nil
end

local function v66(p9)
	local Character = LocalPlayer.Character

	if not Character or not p9 or not p9.Character then
		return
	end

	local HumanoidRootPart = Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	local Head = p9.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
	local Humanoid = Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

	if not HumanoidRootPart or not Head or not Humanoid then
		return
	end

	Humanoid.Sit = true
	u59 = true
	connection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		if u59 and p9.Character then
			if p9.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") and LocalPlayer.Character then
				HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = p9.Character.Head.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 1.2, 0)
				HumanoidRootPart.Velocity = Vector3.zero
				HumanoidRootPart.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero

				return
			end
		end

		if connection then
			connection:Disconnect()
			connection = nil
		end
	end)
end
local function v71()
	u59 = false

	if connection then
		connection:Disconnect()
		connection = nil
	end

	local Character = LocalPlayer.Character

	if Character then
		local Humanoid = Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

		if Humanoid then
			Humanoid.Sit = false
		end
	end
end

	end

						if v:IsA("BasePart") then

							HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(HumanoidRootPart.Position, v.Position)
							v97()

							local now = tick()

							while tick() - now < 0.4 and u81 do
								RunService2.Heartbeat:Wait()
							end

							v98()
							task.wait(0.05)
						end
					end
				end

				v98()
			end)
		else
			u81 = false
		end
	end
})

		if u108 then
			task.spawn(function()
				while u108 do
					local LocalPlayer2 = game.Players.LocalPlayer
					local HumanoidRootPart = (LocalPlayer2.Character or LocalPlayer2.CharacterAdded:Wait()):FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					local v111 = workspace:FindFirstChild("001_Lots")
					local v112 = v111 and v111:FindFirstChild(LocalPlayer2.Name .. "House")

						repeat
							local HouseModel = v112.HousePickedByPlayer:FindFirstChild("HouseModel")

							exitTo = nil

							while true do
								HouseModel = HouseModel and v112.HousePickedByPlayer.HouseModel:FindFirstChild("001_BBQ") and v112.HousePickedByPlayer.HouseModel["001_BBQ"]:FindFirstChild("CatchFire")

								if HouseModel and HumanoidRootPart then
									firetouchinterest(HumanoidRootPart, HouseModel, 0)
									task.wait()
									firetouchinterest(HumanoidRootPart, HouseModel, 1)
								end

								task.wait(2)

								if not u108 then
									break
								end

								local LocalPlayer3 = game.Players.LocalPlayer

								HumanoidRootPart = (LocalPlayer3.Character or LocalPlayer3.CharacterAdded:Wait()):FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

								local v116 = workspace:FindFirstChild("001_Lots")

								v112 = v116 and v116:FindFirstChild(LocalPlayer3.Name .. "House")
								HouseModel = v112 and v112:FindFirstChild("HousePickedByPlayer")

								if HouseModel then
									exitTo = 1

									break
								end
							end
						until exitTo ~= 1

						return
					end

					task.wait(2)
				end
			end)
		end
	end

v40:AddSection({ "ESP" })

do
	local u124 = false
	local t2 = {}
	local t3 = {}
	local s1 = "RGB"
	local Players2 = game:GetService("Players")
	local RunService2 = game:GetService("RunService")

	game:GetService("HttpService")
	game:GetService("MarketplaceService")
	game:GetService("TextChatService")
	v40:AddDropdown({
		Name = "Cor do ESP",
		Default = "RGB",
		Options = {
			"RGB",
			"Branco",
			"Preto",
			"Vermelho",
			"Verde",
			"Azul",
			"Amarelo",
			"Rosa",
			"Roxo"
		},
		Callback = function(p17)
			s1 = p17
		end
	})

	local function v130()
		if s1 == "RGB" then
			return Color3.fromHSV(tick() % 5 / 5, 1, 1)
		end

		if s1 == "Preto" then
			return Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
		end

		if s1 == "Branco" then
			return Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
		end

		if s1 == "Vermelho" then
			return Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
		end

		if s1 == "Verde" then
			return Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0)
		end

		if s1 == "Azul" then
			return Color3.fromRGB(0, 170, 255)
		end

		if s1 == "Amarelo" then
			return Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 0)
		end

		if s1 == "Rosa" then
			return Color3.fromRGB(255, 105, 180)
		end

		if s1 == "Roxo" then
			return Color3.fromRGB(128, 0, 128)
		end

		return Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	end
	local function v131(p18)
		if p18 == Players2.LocalPlayer then
			return
		end

		if not u124 then
			return
		end

		local Character = p18.Character

		if Character then
			local Head = Character:FindFirstChild("Head")

			if Head then
				if t2[p18] then
					t2[p18]:Destroy()
				end

				local BillboardGui = Instance.new("BillboardGui")

				BillboardGui.Name = "ESP_Billboard"
				BillboardGui.Parent = Head
				BillboardGui.Adornee = Head
				BillboardGui.Size = UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 50)
				BillboardGui.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
				BillboardGui.AlwaysOnTop = true

				local TextLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")

				TextLabel.Name = "TextLabel"
				TextLabel.Parent = BillboardGui
				TextLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
				TextLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
				TextLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5
				TextLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
				TextLabel.TextSize = 14
				TextLabel.Text = p18.Name .. " | " .. p18.AccountAge .. " dias"
				TextLabel.TextColor3 = v130()
				t2[p18] = BillboardGui
			end
		end
	end
	local function v136(p19)
		if t2[p19] then
			t2[p19]:Destroy()
			t2[p19] = nil
		end
	end

	v40:AddToggle({
		Name = "ESP Ativado",
		Description = "Mostra nome e idade da conta dos jogadores.",
		Default = false
	}):Callback(function(p20)
		u124 = p20

		if u124 then
			for _, player in pairs(Players2:GetPlayers()) do

			local connection2 = RunService2.Heartbeat:Connect(function()

						if v144 and v144:FindFirstChild("TextLabel") then
							v144.TextLabel.TextColor3 = v130()
						end
					end
				end
			end)

			table.insert(t3, connection2)

			local connection3 = Players2.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player)
				v131(player)

				local connection3 = player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
					v131(player)
				end)

				table.insert(t3, connection3)
			end)

			table.insert(t3, connection3)

			local connection4 = Players2.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
				v136(player)
			end)

			table.insert(t3, connection4)
		else
			for _, player in pairs(Players2:GetPlayers()) do
				v136(player)
			end

			for _, v in pairs(t3) do
				v:Disconnect()
			end

			t3 = {}
			t2 = {}
		end
	end)
end

	Callback = function()
		if not Target then
			return
		end

		local Character = Players2.LocalPlayer.Character
		local v215 = Players2:FindFirstChild(Target)

		if v215 and v215.Character then
			local Humanoid = Character and Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			local Humanoid2 = v215.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

			if Humanoid and Humanoid2 then
				local appliedDescription = Humanoid:GetAppliedDescription()

				for _, v in ipairs(appliedDescription:GetAccessories(true)) do
					if v.AssetId and tonumber(v.AssetId) then
						Remotes.Wear:InvokeServer(tonumber(v.AssetId))
						task.wait(0.2)
					end
				end

				if tonumber(appliedDescription.Shirt) then
					Remotes.Wear:InvokeServer(tonumber(appliedDescription.Shirt))
					task.wait(0.2)
				end

				if tonumber(appliedDescription.Pants) then
					Remotes.Wear:InvokeServer(tonumber(appliedDescription.Pants))
					task.wait(0.2)
				end

				if tonumber(appliedDescription.Face) then
					Remotes.Wear:InvokeServer(tonumber(appliedDescription.Face))
					task.wait(0.2)
				end

				local appliedDescription2 = Humanoid2:GetAppliedDescription()

				Remotes.ChangeCharacterBody:InvokeServer(unpack({ {
					appliedDescription2.Torso,
					appliedDescription2.RightArm,
					appliedDescription2.LeftArm,
					appliedDescription2.RightLeg,
					appliedDescription2.LeftLeg,
					appliedDescription2.Head
				} }))
				task.wait(0.5)

				if tonumber(appliedDescription2.Shirt) then
					Remotes.Wear:InvokeServer(tonumber(appliedDescription2.Shirt))
					task.wait(0.3)
				end

				if tonumber(appliedDescription2.Pants) then
					Remotes.Wear:InvokeServer(tonumber(appliedDescription2.Pants))
					task.wait(0.3)
				end

				if tonumber(appliedDescription2.Face) then
					Remotes.Wear:InvokeServer(tonumber(appliedDescription2.Face))
					task.wait(0.3)
				end

				for _, v in ipairs(appliedDescription2:GetAccessories(true)) do
					if v.AssetId and tonumber(v.AssetId) then
						Remotes.Wear:InvokeServer(tonumber(v.AssetId))
						task.wait(0.3)
					end
				end

				local BodyColors = v215.Character:FindFirstChild("Body Colors")

				if BodyColors then
					Remotes.ChangeBodyColor:FireServer(tostring(BodyColors.HeadColor))
					task.wait(0.3)
				end

				if tonumber(appliedDescription2.IdleAnimation) then
					Remotes.Wear:InvokeServer(tonumber(appliedDescription2.IdleAnimation))
					task.wait(0.3)

					local PlayersBag = v215:FindFirstChild("PlayersBag")

					if PlayersBag then
						if PlayersBag:FindFirstChild("RPName") and PlayersBag.RPName.Value ~= "" then
							Remotes.RPNameText:FireServer("RolePlayName", PlayersBag.RPName.Value)
							task.wait(0.3)
						end

						if PlayersBag:FindFirstChild("RPBio") and PlayersBag.RPBio.Value ~= "" then
							Remotes.RPNameText:FireServer("RolePlayBio", PlayersBag.RPBio.Value)
							task.wait(0.3)
						end

						if PlayersBag:FindFirstChild("RPNameColor") then
							Remotes.RPNameColor:FireServer("PickingRPNameColor", PlayersBag.RPNameColor.Value)
							task.wait(0.3)
						end

						if PlayersBag:FindFirstChild("RPBioColor") then
							Remotes.RPNameColor:FireServer("PickingRPBioColor", PlayersBag.RPBioColor.Value)
							task.wait(0.3)
						end
					end
				else
					local PlayersBag = v215:FindFirstChild("PlayersBag")

					if PlayersBag then
						if PlayersBag:FindFirstChild("RPName") and PlayersBag.RPName.Value ~= "" then
							Remotes.RPNameText:FireServer("RolePlayName", PlayersBag.RPName.Value)
							task.wait(0.3)
						end

						if PlayersBag:FindFirstChild("RPBio") and PlayersBag.RPBio.Value ~= "" then
							Remotes.RPNameText:FireServer("RolePlayBio", PlayersBag.RPBio.Value)
							task.wait(0.3)
						end

						if PlayersBag:FindFirstChild("RPNameColor") then
							Remotes.RPNameColor:FireServer("PickingRPNameColor", PlayersBag.RPNameColor.Value)
							task.wait(0.3)
						end

						if PlayersBag:FindFirstChild("RPBioColor") then
							Remotes.RPNameColor:FireServer("PickingRPBioColor", PlayersBag.RPBioColor.Value)
							task.wait(0.3)

							return
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
})

			0,
			0
		} }

		end)

			Remotes2:WaitForChild("Wear"):InvokeServer(111858803548721)
		end)

		local HumanoidDescription = Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and Character.Humanoid:FindFirstChild("HumanoidDescription")
		local t10 = {}

		if HumanoidDescription then
			for _, v in ipairs({
				"BackAccessory",
				"FaceAccessory",
				"FrontAccessory",
				"NeckAccessory",
				"HatAccessory",
				"HairAccessory",
				"ShouldersAccessory",
				"WaistAccessory",
				"GraphicTShirt"
			}) do
				local v235 = HumanoidDescription[v]

				if v235 and v235 ~= "" then
					for match in string.gmatch(v235, "%d+") do
						table.insert(t10, tonumber(match))
					end
				end
			end
		end

		for _, v in ipairs(t10) do
			task.spawn(function()
				pcall(function()
					Remotes2:WaitForChild("Wear"):InvokeServer(v)
				end)
			end)
		end

		local t11 = { 14488197116 }

		pcall(function()
			Remotes2:WaitForChild("Wear"):InvokeServer(unpack(t11))
		end)
	end
})

		"Perna Esquerda",
		"Cabeça"
	}

	local function v245(p27)
		local v246 = t12[p27]
		local Character = LocalPlayer3.Character
		local Humanoid = Character and Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

		if not Humanoid then
			return
		end

		local appliedDescription = Humanoid:GetAppliedDescription()
		local t14 = { {
			appliedDescription.Torso,
			appliedDescription.RightArm,
			appliedDescription.LeftArm,
			appliedDescription.RightLeg,
			appliedDescription.LeftLeg,
			appliedDescription.Head
		} }

		if v246 then
			t14[1][p27] = tonumber(v246) or v246
		end

		Remotes2:WaitForChild("ChangeCharacterBody"):InvokeServer(unpack(t14))
		print("Equipado:", t13[p27], "→", v246)
	end

	for k, v in pairs(t13) do
		v41:AddTextBox({
			Name = v,
			Description = "Coloque o ID para " .. v,
			PlaceholderText = "Digite o ID",
			Callback = function(p28)
				t12[k] = tonumber(p28) or p28
			end
		})
		v41:AddButton({
			Name = "Equipar " .. v,
			Callback = function()
				v245(k)
			end
		})
	end
end

v42:AddSection({ "funções de casa" })

do
	local u253
	local v254
	local u255 = false
	local Workspace3 = game:GetService("Workspace")
	local LocalPlayer3 = game.Players.LocalPlayer
	local vector3 = Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)

	local function v259()
		for _, descendant in ipairs(Workspace3:GetDescendants()) do
			if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
				if descendant.Name:match("^BannedBlock%d*$") then
					descendant:Destroy()
				end
			end
		end
	end
	local function v262()
		while u255 do
			pcall(v259)
			task.wait(1)
		end
	end

	v42:AddButton({
		Name = " Desbanir das Casas",
		Callback = function()
			v259()
		end
	})
	v42:AddToggle({
		Name = " Auto Unban Casas",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(p29)
			u255 = p29

			if u255 then
				task.spawn(v262)
			end
		end
	})

	local function v263()
		local t15 = {}
		local v265 = Workspace3:FindFirstChild("001_Lots")

		if v265 then
			for _, child in ipairs(v265:GetChildren()) do
				if child:IsA("Model") and child.Name ~= "For Sale" then
					table.insert(t15, child.Name)
				end
			end
		end

		return t15
	end

	local v268 = v42:AddDropdown({
		Name = "Escolher Casa",
		Options = v263(),
		Default = "...",
		Callback = function(p30)
			u253 = p30

			if v254 then
				v254:Set(false)
			end
		end
	})

	v42:AddButton({
		Name = " Atualizar Casas",
		Callback = function()
			local v269 = v263()

			if v268 and v268.Set then
				v268:Set(v269)
			end
		end
	})
	v42:AddButton({
		Name = " TP para Cofre",
		Callback = function()
			local v270 = Workspace3:FindFirstChild("001_Lots"):FindFirstChild(tostring(u253))

			if v270 and v270:FindFirstChild("HousePickedByPlayer") then
				local v271 = v270.HousePickedByPlayer.HouseModel:FindFirstChild("001_Safe")

				if v271 and LocalPlayer3.Character then
					LocalPlayer3.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(v271.WorldPivot.Position)
				end
			end
		end
	})
	v42:AddButton({
		Name = " teleporta para Casa",
		Callback = function()
			if not u253 then
				return
			end

			local HumanoidRootPart = LocalPlayer3.Character and LocalPlayer3.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			local v273 = Workspace3:FindFirstChild("001_Lots")
			local v274 = v273 and v273:FindFirstChild(u253)
			local BuyHouse = v274 and v274:FindFirstChild("BuyHouse")

			if HumanoidRootPart and BuyHouse and BuyHouse:IsA("BasePart") then
				HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(BuyHouse.Position + vector3)
			end
		end
	})
	v254 = v42:AddToggle({
		Name = " Atravessar Porta da casa selecionanda",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(p31)
			local v276 = Workspace3:FindFirstChild("001_Lots"):FindFirstChild(tostring(u253))

			if v276 and v276:FindFirstChild("HousePickedByPlayer") then
				local v277 = v276.HousePickedByPlayer.HouseModel:FindFirstChild("001_HouseDoors")

				if v277 and v277:FindFirstChild("HouseDoorFront") then
					for _, child in ipairs(v277.HouseDoorFront:GetChildren()) do
						if child:IsA("BasePart") then
							child.CanCollide = not p31
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	})
	v42:AddToggle({
		Name = "Loop Tocar Campainha",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(p32)
			getgenv().LoopCampainha = p32
			task.spawn(function()
				while getgenv().LoopCampainha do
					local v280 = Workspace3["001_Lots"]:FindFirstChild(tostring(u253))

					if v280 then
						if v280:FindFirstChild("HousePickedByPlayer") then
							local v281 = v280.HousePickedByPlayer.HouseModel:FindFirstChild("001_DoorBell")

							if v281 then
								if v281:FindFirstChild("TouchBell") then
									fireclickdetector(v281.TouchBell:FindFirstChildOfClass("ClickDetector"))
								end
							end
						end
					end

					task.wait(0.5)
				end
			end)
		end
	})
	v42:AddToggle({
		Name = "Loop Bater na Porta",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(p33)
			getgenv().LoopBater = p33
			task.spawn(function()
				while getgenv().LoopBater do
					local v282 = Workspace3["001_Lots"]:FindFirstChild(tostring(u253))

					if v282 then
						if v282:FindFirstChild("HousePickedByPlayer") then
							local v283 = v282.HousePickedByPlayer.HouseModel:FindFirstChild("001_HouseDoors")
							local Knock = v283 and v283:FindFirstChild("HouseDoorFront") and v283.HouseDoorFront:FindFirstChild("Knock")

							if Knock then
								if Knock:FindFirstChild("TouchBell") then
									fireclickdetector(Knock.TouchBell:FindFirstChildOfClass("ClickDetector"))
								end
							end
						end
					end

					task.wait(0.5)
				end
			end)
		end
	})
end

		TextLabel2.Text = "Status: Off"
		TextLabel2.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 105, 180)
		TextLabel2.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
		TextLabel2.TextScaled = true

		local TextLabel3 = Instance.new("TextLabel", Frame)

		TextLabel3.Position = UDim2.new(0.05, 0, 0.35, 0)
		TextLabel3.Size = UDim2.new(0.3, 0, 0.1, 0)
		TextLabel3.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		TextLabel3.Text = "Velocidade:"
		TextLabel3.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 105, 180)
		TextLabel3.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
		TextLabel3.TextScaled = true

		local TextBox = Instance.new("TextBox", Frame)

		TextBox.Position = UDim2.new(0.4, 0, 0.35, 0)
		TextBox.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.1, 0)
		TextBox.Text = "50"
		TextBox.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 105, 180)
		TextBox.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
		TextBox.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 35)
		TextBox.TextScaled = true

		local TextButton3 = Instance.new("TextButton", Frame)

		TextButton3.Position = UDim2.new(0.05, 0, 0.5, 0)
		TextButton3.Size = UDim2.new(0.9, 0, 0.12, 0)
		TextButton3.Text = "Ativar Fly"
		TextButton3.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 105, 180)
		TextButton3.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
		TextButton3.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 25)
		TextButton3.TextScaled = true

		local TextButton4 = Instance.new("TextButton", Frame)

		TextButton4.Position = UDim2.new(0.05, 0, 0.65, 0)
		TextButton4.Size = UDim2.new(0.9, 0, 0.12, 0)
		TextButton4.Text = "Desativar Fly"
		TextButton4.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 105, 180)
		TextButton4.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
		TextButton4.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 25)
		TextButton4.TextScaled = true
		TextButton4.Visible = false

		local Frame3 = Instance.new("Frame", ScreenGui)

		Frame3.Position = UDim2.new(0.12, 0, 0.7, 0)
		Frame3.Size = UDim2.new(0, 100, 0, 100)
		Frame3.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(15, 15, 15)
		Frame3.Visible = false

		local TextButton5 = Instance.new("TextButton", Frame3)

		TextButton5.Position = UDim2.new(0.25, 0, 0, 0)
		TextButton5.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
		TextButton5.Text = "↑"
		TextButton5.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 105, 180)
		TextButton5.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
		TextButton5.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 30)
		TextButton5.TextScaled = true

		local TextButton6 = Instance.new("TextButton", Frame3)

		TextButton6.Position = UDim2.new(0.25, 0, 0.5, 0)
		TextButton6.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
		TextButton6.Text = "↓"
		TextButton6.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 105, 180)
		TextButton6.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
		TextButton6.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 30)
		TextButton6.TextScaled = true

		local function v299()
			local Character = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character

			Character = Character and game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

			if not Character then
				return
			end

			local BodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity", Character)

			BodyVelocity.Name = "LolytaFlyBV"
			BodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(100000, 100000, 100000)
			BodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.zero

			local BodyGyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro", Character)

			BodyGyro.Name = "LolytaFlyGyro"
			BodyGyro.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(100000, 100000, 100000)
			BodyGyro.P = 100000
			BodyGyro.CFrame = workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame
			game:GetService("RunService").RenderStepped:Connect(function()
				if BodyGyro.Parent then
					BodyGyro.CFrame = workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame
				end
			end)
		end
		local function v303()
			local HumanoidRootPart = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character and game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

			if not HumanoidRootPart then
				return
			end

			if HumanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild("LolytaFlyBV") then
				HumanoidRootPart.LolytaFlyBV:Destroy()
			end

			if HumanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild("LolytaFlyGyro") then
				HumanoidRootPart.LolytaFlyGyro:Destroy()
			end
		end

		TextButton3.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			TextLabel2.Text = "Status: On"
			TextButton3.Visible = false
			TextButton4.Visible = true
			Frame3.Visible = true
			v299()
		end)
		TextButton4.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			TextLabel2.Text = "Status: Off"
			TextButton3.Visible = true
			TextButton4.Visible = false
			Frame3.Visible = false
			v303()
		end)

		local function v305(p34)
			local HumanoidRootPart = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character and game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

			if not HumanoidRootPart then
				return
			end

			local velocity = workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.LookVector * (tonumber(TextBox.Text) or 50) * p34

			if HumanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild("LolytaFlyBV") then
				HumanoidRootPart.LolytaFlyBV.Velocity = velocity
			end
		end

		TextButton5.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			v305(1)
		end)
		TextButton6.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			v305(-1)
		end)

		local u308 = false

		TextButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			u308 = not u308

			for _, child in ipairs(Frame:GetChildren()) do
				if child:IsA("GuiObject") and child ~= Frame2 then
					child.Visible = not u308
				end
			end

			Frame.Size = u308 and UDim2.new(0, 300, 0, 30) or UDim2.new(0, 300, 0, 220)
		end)
		TextButton2.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			ScreenGui:Destroy()
		end)
	end
})

do
	local Players3 = game:GetService("Players")
	local Workspace3 = game:GetService("Workspace")
	local StarterGui3 = game:GetService("StarterGui")

	game:GetService("RunService")

	local LocalPlayer3 = Players3.LocalPlayer

	v43:AddSection({ "Speed e Turbo (Meu Carro)" })

	local s2 = ""
	local s3 = "11"

	local function v317()
		local Vehicles = Workspace3:FindFirstChild("Vehicles")

		if not Vehicles then
			return nil
		end

		local v319 = Vehicles:FindFirstChild(LocalPlayer3.Name .. "Car")

		if not v319 then
			return nil
		end

		local Seats = v319:FindFirstChild("Seats")

		if Seats then
			return Seats:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat")
		end

		return nil
	end

	v43:AddTextBox({
		Name = "Velocidade Máxima",
		Description = "Defina a velocidade máxima do seu carro.",
		PlaceholderText = "Ex: 200",
		Callback = function(p35)
			s2 = p35
		end
	})
	v43:AddButton({
		Name = "Aplicar Velocidade",
		Callback = function()
			local v321 = v317()

			if not v321 then
				StarterGui3:SetCore("SendNotification", {
					Title = "Erro",
					Text = "Seu carro não foi encontrado."
				})

				return
			end

			local value = tonumber(s2)

			if not value then
				StarterGui3:SetCore("SendNotification", {
					Title = "Erro",
					Text = "Valor de velocidade inválido."
				})

				return
			end

			local MaxSpeed = v321:FindFirstChild("MaxSpeed")

			if MaxSpeed and MaxSpeed:IsA("NumberValue") then
				MaxSpeed.Value = value
				StarterGui3:SetCore("SendNotification", {
					Title = "Sucesso",
					Text = "Velocidade definida para " .. value
				})
			else
				StarterGui3:SetCore("SendNotification", {
					Title = "Erro",
					Text = "Não encontrei o MaxSpeed no VehicleSeat."
				})
			end
		end
	})
	v43:AddTextBox({
		Name = "Valor do Turbo",
		Description = "Defina o valor do turbo.",
		PlaceholderText = "Ex: 11.3",
		Callback = function(p36)
			s3 = p36
		end
	})
	v43:AddButton({
		Name = "Aplicar Turbo",
		Callback = function()
			local v324 = v317()

			if not v324 then
				StarterGui3:SetCore("SendNotification", {
					Title = "Erro",
					Text = "Seu carro não foi encontrado."
				})

				return
			end

			local Turbo = v324:FindFirstChild("Turbo")

			if Turbo and Turbo:IsA("StringValue") then
				Turbo.Value = tostring(s3)
				StarterGui3:SetCore("SendNotification", {
					Title = "Sucesso",
					Text = "Turbo definido para " .. Turbo.Value
				})
			else
				StarterGui3:SetCore("SendNotification", {
					Title = "Erro",
					Text = "Não encontrei o Turbo no VehicleSeat."
				})
			end
		end
	})
end

v43:AddSection({ "Interação com Veículos do Servidor" })
v43:AddParagraph({
	Title = "use o void protection",
	Content = "Ative a proteção contra o vazio (Tab 2) antes de usar as funções."
})

do
	local u326
	local u327 = false
	local u328 = false
	local t16 = {}
	local v330

	local function v331(p37, p38)
		local function v332()
			StarterGui2:SetCore("SendNotification", {
				Title = p37,
				Text = p38,
				Duration = 5
			})
		end

		pcall(v332)
	end
	local function v333(p39)
		if p39 then
			if p39:IsA("Model") then
				local Body = p39:FindFirstChild("Body")

				return Body and Body:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat") or p39:FindFirstChildOfClass("VehicleSeat", true)
			end
		end

		return nil
	end
	local function v335(p40, p41)
		if p40 then
			if p40:IsA("Model") then
				pcall(function()
					p40:PivotTo(p41)
				end)

				return
			end
		end
	end
	local function v336(p42)
		local Humanoid = LocalPlayer2.Character and LocalPlayer2.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

		if not Humanoid then
			return
		end

		Humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, p42)
		Humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, p42)
		u327 = not p42
	end
	local function v338(p43)
		local Humanoid = LocalPlayer2.Character and LocalPlayer2.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		local HumanoidRootPart = Humanoid and LocalPlayer2.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

		if not HumanoidRootPart then
			return false
		end

		Humanoid.Sit = false
		task.wait(0.1)
		HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = p43.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 5, 0)
		task.wait(0.1)
		p43:Sit(Humanoid)
		task.wait(0.2)

		return Humanoid.SeatPart == p43
	end
	local function v341(p44)
		local Humanoid = LocalPlayer2.Character and LocalPlayer2.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

		if Humanoid then
			Humanoid.Sit = false
		end

		task.wait(0.1)

		if p44 then
			if LocalPlayer2.Character then
				pcall(function()
					LocalPlayer2.Character:PivotTo(CFrame.new(p44))
				end)
			end
		end
	end
	local function v343()
		local t17 = {}
		local Vehicles = Workspace2:FindFirstChild("Vehicles")

		if Vehicles then
			for _, child in ipairs(Vehicles:GetChildren()) do
				if child.Name:match("Car$") and v333(child) then
					table.insert(t17, child.Name)
				end
			end
		end

		table.sort(t17)

		return #t17 > 0 and t17 or { "Nenhum carro no servidor" }
	end
	local function v348(p45, p46)
		local v349 = v333(p45)

		if not v349 then
			v331("Erro", "Assento do veículo '" .. p45.Name .. "' não encontrado.")

			return
		end

		if v349.Occupant then
			v331("Aviso", "Veículo '" .. p45.Name .. "' está ocupado.")

			return
		end

		local Position = LocalPlayer2.Character and LocalPlayer2.Character.PrimaryPart and LocalPlayer2.Character.PrimaryPart.Position

		if not Position then
			v331("Erro", "Personagem não encontrado.")

			return
		end

		v336(false)

		if v338(v349) then
			if p46 == "destroy" then
				v335(p45, CFrame.new(0, -2000, 0))
				v331("Sucesso", "Veículo '" .. p45.Name .. "' destruído.")
			elseif p46 == "bring" then
				v335(p45, CFrame.new(Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 5)))
				v331("Sucesso", "Veículo '" .. p45.Name .. "' foi trazido até você.")
			end

			v341(Position)
		else
			v331("Erro", "Não foi possível entrar no veículo '" .. p45.Name .. "'.")
		end

		v336(true)
	end
	local function v351()
		if v330 and v330.Set then
			v330:Set(v343())
		end
	end

	v330 = v43:AddDropdown({
		Name = "Selecionar Veículo",
		Description = "Escolha um carro para interagir.",
		Options = v343(),
		Default = "Nenhum carro no servidor",
		Callback = function(p47)
			if p47 and type(p47) == "string" then
				if p47:find("Nenhum carro") then
					u326 = nil
				else
					u326 = p47
				end
			else
				u326 = nil
			end
		end
	})
	v43:AddButton({
		"Destruir Carro Selecionado",
		function()
			if not u326 then
				v331("Aviso", "Nenhum carro selecionado.")

				return
			end

			local v352 = Workspace2.Vehicles:FindFirstChild(u326)

			if v352 then
				v348(v352, "destroy")
			end
		end
	})
	v43:AddButton({
		"Trazer Carro Selecionado",
		function()
			if not u326 then
				v331("Aviso", "Nenhum carro selecionado.")

				return
			end

			local v353 = Workspace2.Vehicles:FindFirstChild(u326)

			if v353 then
				v348(v353, "bring")
			end
		end
	})
	v43:AddToggle({
		Name = "Ver Câmera do Carro Selecionado",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(p48)
			if p48 then
				if not u326 then
					v331("Aviso", "Nenhum carro selecionado.")

					return false
				end

				local v354 = Workspace2.Vehicles:FindFirstChild(u326)
				local v355 = v354 and v333(v354)

				if not v355 then
					v331("Erro", "Não foi possível focar no carro selecionado.")

					return false
				end

				t16.Subject = Workspace2.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject
				t16.Type = Workspace2.CurrentCamera.CameraType
				Workspace2.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = v355
				Workspace2.CurrentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Follow
				v331("Câmera", "Focando em '" .. u326 .. "'")
			elseif t16.Subject then
				Workspace2.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = t16.Subject
				Workspace2.CurrentCamera.CameraType = t16.Type
				t16 = {}
				v331("Câmera", "Câmera restaurada.")
			end
		end
	})
	v43:AddButton({
		"Trazer Todos os Carros",
		function()
			local v356 = v343()

			if v356[1]:find("Nenhum carro") then
				v331("Aviso", "Nenhum carro no servidor.")

				return
			end

			task.spawn(function()
				for _, v in ipairs(v356) do
					local v359 = Workspace2.Vehicles:FindFirstChild(v)

					if v359 then
						v348(v359, "bring")
						task.wait(1.5)
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	})
	v43:AddToggle({
		Name = "Matar Todos os Carros (Loop)",
		Description = "Entra em cada carro vazio e o joga no void.",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(p49)
			u328 = p49

			if p49 then
				v331("Aviso", "Loop de destruição de carros ativado.")
				task.spawn(function()
					while u328 do
						local v360 = v343()

						if not v360[1]:find("Nenhum carro") then
							for _, v in ipairs(v360) do
								if not u328 then
									break
								end

								local v363 = Workspace2.Vehicles:FindFirstChild(v)

								if v363 then
									v348(v363, "destroy")
									task.wait(1.5)
								end
							end
						end

						task.wait(5)
					end

					v331("Aviso", "Loop de destruição de carros desativado.")
				end)
			end
		end
	})
	LocalPlayer2.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
		task.wait(1)
		v336(not u327)
	end)

	if Workspace2:FindFirstChild("Vehicles") then
		Workspace2.Vehicles.ChildAdded:Connect(v351)
		Workspace2.Vehicles.ChildRemoved:Connect(v351)
	end
end

	local function v365(p52)
		local n1 = 0

		for match in p52:upper():gmatch(".") do
			local v368 = t18[match]

			if v368 then
				for _, v in ipairs(v368) do
					n1 += v:gsub("0", ""):len()
				end
			end
		end

		return n1
	end

	local function createFrame(p53, p54, p55, p56)

		Frame3.BackgroundTransparency = p56
		Frame3.BorderSizePixel = 0
		Frame3.ZIndex = 3
		Instance.new("UICorner", Frame3).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
		task.spawn(function()
			while Frame3.Parent do
				local v386 = Frame3.Position.X.Scale - p55

				end

				Frame3.BackgroundTransparency = p56 + math.sin(tick() * 3) * 0.2
				task.wait(0.03)
			end
		end)

		return Frame3
	end

	local function v467()
		local n10 = 0

		for _, v in ipairs({
			LocalPlayer3.Backpack,
			LocalPlayer3.Character
		}) do
			for _, child in ipairs(v:GetChildren()) do
				if child:IsA("Tool") then
					n10 += 1
				end
			end
		end

		return n10
	end
	local function v473(p57)
		local n11 = 0

		for match in p57:upper():gmatch(".") do
			local v476 = t18[match]

			if v476 then
				for _, v in ipairs(v476) do
					n11 += v:gsub("0", ""):len()
				end
			else
				warn("Invalid character:", match)
			end
		end

		return n11
	end

	local v480 = v473(v479)

	local function v481(p58)
		for i = 1, 100 do
			Frame10.Size = UDim2.new(i / 100, 0, 1, 0)

			local v483 = v467()
			local v484 = math.floor(v483 / v480 * 100)

			TextLabel5.Text = string.format("Coletando: %d/%d (%d%%)", v483, v480, v484)

			if v484 < 30 then
				TextLabel5.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 100, 100)
				UIStroke6.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 100, 100)
			elseif v484 < 70 then
				TextLabel5.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 100)
				UIStroke6.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 100)
			else
				TextLabel5.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(100, 255, 100)
				UIStroke6.Color = Color3.fromRGB(100, 255, 100)
			end

			task.wait(0.03)
		end

		TextLabel5.Text = string.format(" Completo: %d/%d tools!", v480, v480)
		TextLabel5.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(100, 255, 100)
		UIStroke6.Color = Color3.fromRGB(100, 255, 100)
		task.wait(1)
		p58()
	end

		local Paper = ({

			warn("Not enough tools, required: " .. v480 .. ", Found: " .. #t25)

					end
				end

				n13 = n13 + #v505[1] + 1
			end
		end

		game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid").Sit = false
		v487(Position)
		task.wait(3)

		for k, v in pairs(t26) do
			k.Grip = v
		end

		task.wait(3)

		for i = 1, v480 do
			if t25[i] then
				t25[i].Parent = LocalPlayer3.Character
			end
		end

		toolsUsados = 0
		getgenv().toolsUsados = 0
		Sound:Stop()
		Sound:Destroy()
		ScreenGui:Destroy()
		getgenv()._Running = false
	end)
end

	local function v530(p62)
		local workspace_ = workspace

		for match in string.gmatch(p62, "[^%.]+") do

				continue
			end

			return nil
		end

	local function v607(p82)

							return

						local v614 = geniter(v611, v612, v613)

								if v:IsA("BasePart") then
									v.Velocity = Vector3.zero

									break

								HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(v608.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0))
							end)

								return
							end

							Humanoid.Sit = false
							Humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
							task.wait(0.5)

							local v620, v621, v622 = ipairs(LocalPlayer3.Character:GetDescendants())

							for _, v2 in v620, v621, v622 do
								if v2:IsA("BasePart") then
									v2.Anchored = false
									v2.Velocity = Vector3.zero
									v2.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
								end
							end

							return
						end

						local ok = pcall(function()
							HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(v608.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0))
						end)

						if not ok then
							return
						end

						Humanoid.Sit = false
						Humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
						task.wait(0.5)

						local v627, v628, v629 = ipairs(LocalPlayer3.Character:GetDescendants())

						for _, v in v627, v628, v629 do
							if v:IsA("BasePart") then
								v.Anchored = false
								v.Velocity = Vector3.zero

					end

		end

	local function v641()
		local Backpack = LocalPlayer3:WaitForChild("Backpack")
		local Couch = Backpack:FindFirstChild("Couch")
		local Couch2

		if Couch then
			Couch.Parent = LocalPlayer3.Character

			local now = tick()

			repeat
				Couch2 = LocalPlayer3.Character and LocalPlayer3.Character:FindFirstChild("Couch")

				if not Couch2 then
					Couch2 = Backpack:FindFirstChild("Couch")

					if Couch2 then
						Couch2.Parent = LocalPlayer3.Character
					end
				end

				task.wait(0.05)
			until Couch2 or tick() - now > 1
		else
			local Couch3 = LocalPlayer3.Character and LocalPlayer3.Character:FindFirstChild("Couch")

			if not Couch3 then
				local t35 = {
					"PickingTools",
					"Couch"
				}

				pcall(function()
					ReplicatedStorage2:WaitForChild("RE"):WaitForChild("1Too1l"):InvokeServer(unpack(t35))
				end)

				local now = tick()

				while true do
					Couch3 = Backpack:FindFirstChild("Couch")
					task.wait(0.05)

					if Couch3 then
						break
					elseif tick() - now > 1.5 then
						break
					end
				end

				if not Couch3 then
					return false
				end
			end

			Couch3.Parent = LocalPlayer3.Character

			local now = tick()

			repeat
				Couch2 = LocalPlayer3.Character and LocalPlayer3.Character:FindFirstChild("Couch")

				if not Couch2 then
					Couch2 = Backpack:FindFirstChild("Couch")

					if Couch2 then
						Couch2.Parent = LocalPlayer3.Character
					end
				end

				task.wait(0.05)
			until Couch2 or tick() - now > 1
		end

		return Couch2 ~= nil
	end

	local function v652(p88)
		if not p88 or not p88.Character or not LocalPlayer3.Character then
			return
		end

		local Character = LocalPlayer3.Character
		local Humanoid = Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		local HumanoidRootPart = Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

		if not Humanoid or not HumanoidRootPart then
			return
		end

		savedPosition = HumanoidRootPart.Position
		pcall(function()
			HumanoidRootPart.Anchored = true
			HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(Vector3.new(1181.83, 76.08, -1158.83))
			task.wait(0.2)
			HumanoidRootPart.Velocity = Vector3.zero
			HumanoidRootPart.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
			HumanoidRootPart.Anchored = false

			if Humanoid then
				Humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
			end
		end)
		task.wait(0.5)
		v592()

		local t36 = { "DeleteAllVehicles" }

		pcall(function()
			ReplicatedStorage2:WaitForChild("RE"):WaitForChild("1Ca1r"):FireServer(unpack(t36))
		end)
		t36 = {
			"PickingCar",
			"SchoolBus"
		}
		pcall(function()
			ReplicatedStorage2:WaitForChild("RE"):WaitForChild("1Ca1r"):FireServer(unpack(t36))
		end)
		task.wait(1)

		local Vehicles = Workspace3:FindFirstChild("Vehicles")

		if not Vehicles then
			return
		end

		local v658 = Vehicles:FindFirstChild(LocalPlayer3.Name .. "Car")

		if not v658 then
			return
		end

		pcall(function()
			HumanoidRootPart.Anchored = true
			HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(Vector3.new(1171.15, 79.45, -1166.2))
			task.wait(0.2)
			HumanoidRootPart.Velocity = Vector3.zero
			HumanoidRootPart.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
			HumanoidRootPart.Anchored = false
			Humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated)
		end)

		local now = tick()

		while true do
			task.wait()

			if tick() - now > 10 then
				return
			elseif Humanoid.Sit then
				break
			end
		end

		for _, descendant in ipairs(v658:GetDescendants()) do
			if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
				descendant.CanCollide = false
				pcall(function()
					descendant:SetNetworkOwner(nil)
				end)
			end
		end

		u584 = true
		connection3 = RunService2.Stepped:Connect(function()
			if not u584 then
				return
			end

			for _, descendant in ipairs(Character:GetDescendants()) do
				if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
					descendant.CanCollide = false
				end
			end
		end)

		local now2 = tick()
		local now3 = tick()

		flingConnection = RunService2.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
			if not u584 then
				return
			end

			local Character2 = p88.Character or p88.CharacterAdded:Wait()
			local HumanoidRootPart2 = Character2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			local Humanoid2 = Character2:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

			if not HumanoidRootPart2 or not Humanoid2 then
				return
			end

			if not HumanoidRootPart or not Humanoid then
				u584 = false

				return
			end

			if tick() - now2 < 0.05 then
				return
			end

			now2 = tick()

			local vector3 = Vector3.new(math.random(-10, 10), 0, math.random(-10, 10))

			pcall(function()
				v658:PivotTo(CFrame.new(HumanoidRootPart2.Position + vector3) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(Workspace3.DistributedGameTime * 12000), math.rad(Workspace3.DistributedGameTime * 15000), math.rad(Workspace3.DistributedGameTime * 18000)))
			end)

			local v670 = false
			local v671, v672, v673 = ipairs(v658:GetDescendants())

			for _, v in v671, v672, v673 do
				if (v:IsA("Seat") or v:IsA("VehicleSeat")) and v.Name ~= "VehicleSeat" and v.Occupant == Humanoid2 then
					v670 = true

					break
				end
			end

			local t37, HumanoidRootPart3, v678, v679, v680, v681, exitTo, v683, exitTo2, Humanoid3, v686, v687, v688, Humanoid4, v690, v691, v692

			if v670 then
				u584 = false

				if connection3 then
					connection3:Disconnect()
					connection3 = nil
				end

				if flingConnection then
					flingConnection:Disconnect()
					flingConnection = nil
				end

				pcall(function()
					v658:PivotTo(CFrame.new(Vector3.new(-76.6, -401.97, -84.26)))
				end)
				task.wait(0.5)
				v592()
				t37 = { "DeleteAllVehicles" }
				pcall(function()
					ReplicatedStorage2:WaitForChild("RE"):WaitForChild("1Ca1r"):FireServer(unpack(t37))
				end)

				if Character then
					HumanoidRootPart3 = Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

					if HumanoidRootPart3 and savedPosition then
						pcall(function()
							HumanoidRootPart3.Anchored = true
							HumanoidRootPart3.CFrame = CFrame.new(savedPosition + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0))
							task.wait(0.2)
							HumanoidRootPart3.Velocity = Vector3.zero
							HumanoidRootPart3.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
							HumanoidRootPart3.Anchored = false

							if Humanoid then
								Humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
							end
						end)
					end
				end

				if Character then
					v678, v679, v680 = ipairs(Character:GetDescendants())
					v681 = geniter(v678, v679, v680)
					exitTo = nil

					for _, v in v678, v679, v680 do
						if v:IsA("BasePart") then
							v683 = v
							exitTo = 1

							break
						end
					end

					if exitTo == 1 then
						repeat
							v683.CanCollide = true
							v683.Velocity = Vector3.zero
							v683.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
							exitTo2 = nil

							while true do
								local v695 = table.pack(v681())

								if not v695[1] then
									break
								end

								v683 = v695[3]

								if v683:IsA("BasePart") then
									exitTo2 = 1

									break
								end
							end
						until exitTo2 ~= 1

						Humanoid3 = Character and Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

						if Humanoid3 then
							Humanoid3:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated, true)
						end

						v686, v687, v688 = ipairs(Workspace3:GetDescendants())

						for _, v in v686, v687, v688 do
							if not v:IsA("Seat") and not v:IsA("VehicleSeat") then
								continue
							end

							v.Disabled = false
						end

						pcall(function()
							ReplicatedStorage2:WaitForChild("RE"):WaitForChild("1Clothe1s"):FireServer("CharacterSizeUp", 1)
						end)
					else
						Humanoid4 = Character and Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

						if Humanoid4 then
							Humanoid4:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated, true)
						end

						v690, v691, v692 = ipairs(Workspace3:GetDescendants())

						for _, v in v690, v691, v692 do
							if v:IsA("Seat") or v:IsA("VehicleSeat") then
								v.Disabled = false
							end
						end

						pcall(function()
							ReplicatedStorage2:WaitForChild("RE"):WaitForChild("1Clothe1s"):FireServer("CharacterSizeUp", 1)
						end)
					end
				else
					Humanoid4 = Character and Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

					if Humanoid4 then
						Humanoid4:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated, true)
					end

					v690, v691, v692 = ipairs(Workspace3:GetDescendants())

					for _, v in v690, v691, v692 do
						if v:IsA("Seat") or v:IsA("VehicleSeat") then
							v.Disabled = false
						end
					end

				end)

				end

							exitTo = 1

							break
						end
					end

			local LocalPlayer4 = game.Players.LocalPlayer
			local Character = LocalPlayer4.Character or LocalPlayer4.CharacterAdded:Wait()
			local Humanoid = Character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
			local HumanoidRootPart = Character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")

			CFrame_ = HumanoidRootPart.CFrame

			if p101 then
				local AirVehicleSpawner = workspace:FindFirstChild("AirVehicleSpawner")

				if not AirVehicleSpawner or not AirVehicleSpawner:FindFirstChild("Button") then
					warn("Spawner ou botão do helicóptero não encontrado.")

					return
				end

				local Button = AirVehicleSpawner.Button

				HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = Button.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
				task.wait(0.4)

				local ClickDetector = Button:FindFirstChildOfClass("ClickDetector")

				if not ClickDetector then
					warn("ClickDetector não encontrado no botão.")

					return
				end

				fireclickdetector(ClickDetector)
				print("🛫 Menu de helicóptero aberto.")
				task.wait(1)

				local SpawnVehicle = workspace:WaitForChild("AirVehicleSpawner"):FindFirstChild("SpawnVehicle")

				if not SpawnVehicle then
					warn("Remote SpawnVehicle não encontrado!")

					return
				end

				print("🚁 Spawnando Helicóptero via remote...")
				SpawnVehicle:InvokeServer(unpack({ "HelicopterPolice" }))

				local Helicopters = workspace:WaitForChild("Helicopters")
				local HelicopterPolice

				for i = 1, 60 do
					HelicopterPolice = Helicopters:FindFirstChild("HelicopterPolice")

					if HelicopterPolice and HelicopterPolice:FindFirstChild("hEL") then
						print("✅ Helicóptero spawnado com sucesso.")

						break
					end

					task.wait(0.2)
				end

				if not HelicopterPolice then
					warn("⛔ Helicóptero não spawnou.")

					return
				end

				local PilotSeat = HelicopterPolice:FindFirstChild("PilotSeat") or HelicopterPolice:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat")

				if not PilotSeat then
					warn("⚠️ Nenhum assento encontrado (nem PilotSeat nem VehicleSeat).")

					return
				end

				if not HelicopterPolice.PrimaryPart then
					HelicopterPolice.PrimaryPart = PilotSeat
				end

				local n14 = 0
				local v861 = Character
				local MoveTo = Character.MoveTo
				local v863 = PilotSeat.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)

				while true do
					MoveTo(v861, v863)
					task.wait(0.05)
					PilotSeat:Sit(Humanoid)
					n14 += 1

					if Humanoid.Sit or n14 > 120 then
						break
					end

					v861 = Character
					MoveTo = Character.MoveTo
					v863 = PilotSeat.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
				end

				if not Humanoid.Sit then
					warn("Falhou em sentar no helicóptero.")

					return
				end

				BodyPosition = Instance.new("BodyPosition", HelicopterPolice.PrimaryPart)
				BodyPosition.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e9, 1e9, 1e9)
				BodyPosition.P = 100000
				BodyPosition.D = 1000

				local v864 = game.Players:FindFirstChild(v847)

				if not v864 then
					warn("Jogador alvo não encontrado.")

					return
				end

				local Character2 = v864.Character or v864.CharacterAdded:Wait()
				local HumanoidRootPart2 = Character2:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				local Humanoid2 = Character2:WaitForChild("Humanoid")

				connection4 = game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat:Connect(function()
					if Character2 and Humanoid2 then
						if not (Humanoid2.Health <= 0) then
							BodyPosition.Position = HumanoidRootPart2.Position + Vector3.new(0, 6, 0)

							return
						end
					end

					if connection4 then
						connection4:Disconnect()
						connection4 = nil
					end

					if BodyPosition then
						BodyPosition:Destroy()
						BodyPosition = nil
					end
				end)
				print("🚨 Jail Heli ativado e seguindo o alvo.")

				return
			end

			if connection4 then
				connection4:Disconnect()
				connection4 = nil
			end

			if BodyPosition then
				BodyPosition:Destroy()
				BodyPosition = nil
			end

			Humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
			task.wait(0.2)
			HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame_
			print("🛑 Jail Heli desativado e posição restaurada.")
		end
	})

	local function v868()
		local Backpack = LocalPlayer3:WaitForChild("Backpack")
		local Couch = Backpack:FindFirstChild("Couch") or LocalPlayer3.Character and LocalPlayer3.Character:FindFirstChild("Couch")

		if not Couch then
			local t39 = {
				"PickingTools",
				"Couch"
			}

			pcall(function()
				ReplicatedStorage2:WaitForChild("RE"):WaitForChild("1Too1l"):InvokeServer(unpack(t39))
			end)

			local now = tick()

			while true do
				Couch = Backpack:FindFirstChild("Couch")
				task.wait(0.05)

				if Couch then
					break
				elseif tick() - now > 1.5 then
					break
				end
			end

			if not Couch then
				return false
			end
		end

		Couch.Parent = LocalPlayer3.Character

		local now = tick()
		local Couch2 = LocalPlayer3.Character and LocalPlayer3.Character:FindFirstChild("Couch")

		if not Couch2 then
			Couch2 = Backpack:FindFirstChild("Couch")

			if Couch2 then
				Couch2.Parent = LocalPlayer3.Character
			end
		end

		task.wait(0.05)

		if not Couch2 then
			while not (tick() - now > 1) do
				Couch2 = LocalPlayer3.Character and LocalPlayer3.Character:FindFirstChild("Couch")

				if not Couch2 then
					Couch2 = Backpack:FindFirstChild("Couch")

					if Couch2 then
						Couch2.Parent = LocalPlayer3.Character
					end
				end

				task.wait(0.05)

				if Couch2 then
					break
				end
			end
		end

		return Couch2 ~= nil
	end

				flingToggle:SetValue(false)

						if Magnitude > 2 then
							v912.CFrame = CFrame.new(HumanoidRootPart.Position + HumanoidRootPart.CFrame.LookVector * math.clamp(Magnitude * 1.5, 10, 35) + Vector3.new(0, math.sin(n15 * 15) * 3 + 3, 0))
						else
							exitTo = 1

							break
						end
					end

						local v922 = math.sin(n15 * 65) * 6 + 3
						local Position = HumanoidRootPart.Position

							v912.CFrame = CFrame.new(Position + vector3New(n16, v922, 0))

							local exitTo2

								n15 += RunService2.Heartbeat:Wait()

								local Magnitude = HumanoidRootPart.Velocity.Magnitude

								if Magnitude > 2 then

								else
									exitTo2 = 1

									break
								end
							end

								break
							end

							v922 = math.sin(n15 * 65) * 6 + 3
							Position = HumanoidRootPart.Position
							vector3New = Vector3.new
						end

								v912:Destroy()
							end)

					elseif v912 and v912.Parent then

			BodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
			BodyVelocity.P = 1e10
			BodyVelocity.Parent = v912

				Humanoid = Character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid")

				if not HumanoidRootPart then
					return
				end

				if not Humanoid then
					return
				end

				getgenv().FlingAtivo = true
				task.spawn(function()
					local n17 = 0
					local exitTo

					while getgenv().FlingAtivo and Character:IsDescendantOf(workspace) and Humanoid.Health > 0 do
						n17 += RunService2.Heartbeat:Wait()

						local Magnitude = HumanoidRootPart.Velocity.Magnitude

						if Magnitude > 2 then
							v912.CFrame = CFrame.new(HumanoidRootPart.Position + HumanoidRootPart.CFrame.LookVector * math.clamp(Magnitude * 1.5, 10, 35) + Vector3.new(0, math.sin(n17 * 15) * 3 + 3, 0))
						else
							exitTo = 1

							break
						end
					end

					if exitTo == 1 then
						local v934 = math.sin(n17 * 65) * 6 + 3
						local Position = HumanoidRootPart.Position
						local vector3New = Vector3.new
						local n18 = 0

						while true do
							v912.CFrame = CFrame.new(Position + vector3New(n18, v934, 0))

							local exitTo2

							while getgenv().FlingAtivo and Character:IsDescendantOf(workspace) and Humanoid.Health > 0 do
								n17 += RunService2.Heartbeat:Wait()

								local Magnitude = HumanoidRootPart.Velocity.Magnitude

								if Magnitude > 2 then
									v912.CFrame = CFrame.new(HumanoidRootPart.Position + HumanoidRootPart.CFrame.LookVector * math.clamp(Magnitude * 1.5, 10, 35) + Vector3.new(0, math.sin(n17 * 15) * 3 + 3, 0))

	local function v940(p105)

				BodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.new(math.random(-10, 10), 50, math.random(-10, 10)).Unit * 10000000 + Vector3.new(0, 5000000, 0)
				BodyVelocity.Parent = HumanoidRootPart2
				task.delay(0.5, function()
					BodyVelocity:Destroy()

					for _, descendant in ipairs(p107.Character:GetDescendants()) do
						if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
							descendant.CanCollide = true
						end
					end
				end)
			end

			local v1055 = false

			for _, descendant in ipairs(v1006:GetDescendants()) do
				if (descendant:IsA("Seat") or descendant:IsA("VehicleSeat")) and descendant.Name ~= "VehicleSeat" and descendant.Occupant == Humanoid2 then
					v1055 = true

					break
				end
			end

			if v1055 or tick() - now2 > 10 then
				u584 = false

				if connection3 then
					connection3:Disconnect()
					connection3 = nil
				end

				if flingConnection then
					flingConnection:Disconnect()
					flingConnection = nil
				end

				pcall(function()
					v1006:PivotTo(CFrame.new(Vector3.new(-59599.73, 2040070.5, -293391.16)))
				end)
				task.wait(0.5)
				v592()

				local t46 = { "DeleteAllVehicles" }

				pcall(function()
					ReplicatedStorage2:WaitForChild("RE"):WaitForChild("1Ca1r"):FireServer(unpack(t46))
				end)

				if Character then
					local HumanoidRootPart3 = Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

					if HumanoidRootPart3 and savedPosition then
						pcall(function()
							HumanoidRootPart3.Anchored = true
							HumanoidRootPart3.CFrame = CFrame.new(savedPosition + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0))
							task.wait(0.2)
							HumanoidRootPart3.Velocity = Vector3.zero
							HumanoidRootPart3.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
							HumanoidRootPart3.Anchored = false

							if Humanoid then
								Humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
							end
						end)
					end

					if Character then
						for _, descendant in ipairs(Character:GetDescendants()) do
							if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
								descendant.CanCollide = true
								descendant.Velocity = Vector3.zero
								descendant.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
							end
						end
					end

					local Humanoid3 = Character and Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

					if Humanoid3 then
						Humanoid3:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated, true)
					end

					for _, descendant in ipairs(Workspace3:GetDescendants()) do
						if descendant:IsA("Seat") or descendant:IsA("VehicleSeat") then
							descendant.Disabled = false
						end
					end

					pcall(function()
						ReplicatedStorage2:WaitForChild("RE"):WaitForChild("1Clothe1s"):FireServer("CharacterSizeUp", 1)
					end)

					if flingToggle then
						flingToggle:Set(false)
					end
				else
					if Character then
						for _, descendant in ipairs(Character:GetDescendants()) do
							if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
								descendant.CanCollide = true
								descendant.Velocity = Vector3.zero
								descendant.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
							end
						end
					end

					end

					for _, descendant in ipairs(Workspace3:GetDescendants()) do
						if descendant:IsA("Seat") or descendant:IsA("VehicleSeat") then
							descendant.Disabled = false
						end
					end

					pcall(function()
						ReplicatedStorage2:WaitForChild("RE"):WaitForChild("1Clothe1s"):FireServer("CharacterSizeUp", 1)
					end)

					if flingToggle then

						return
					end
				end

		end)

		end

		v1826(LocalPlayer3.Character)
	end

do
	local Players4 = game:GetService("Players")
	local StarterGui3 = game:GetService("StarterGui")
	local LocalPlayer3 = Players4.LocalPlayer
	local RunService2 = game:GetService("RunService")
	local u1922 = false
	local t70 = {}
	local t71 = {}

	local function v1925(p167)
		local HeadShot = Enum.ThumbnailType.HeadShot
		local Size420x420 = Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size420x420
		local ok, result = pcall(function()
			return Players4:GetUserThumbnailAsync(p167, HeadShot, Size420x420)
		end)

		return ok and result or ""
	end
	local function v1930(p168)
		if t71[p168.Name] then
			return
		end

		t71[p168.Name] = true

		local v1931 = v1925(p168.UserId)

		pcall(function()
			StarterGui3:SetCore("SendNotification", {
				Title = "Skybox/Flashbang Detectado",
				Text = p168.Name .. " está usando Skybox ou Flashbang geral!",
				Icon = v1931,
				Duration = 5
			})
		end)
	end
	local function v1932(p169)
		if p169 then
			return tostring(p169):match("%d+") == "93224413172183"
		end

		return false
	end
	local function v1933(p170)
		if p170 then
			return tostring(p170):match("%d+") == "115959405843119"
		end

		return false
	end
	local function v1934(p171, p172)
		local u1935 = false
		local Head = p171:FindFirstChild("Head")

		if Head then
			local v1937, v1938, v1939 = ipairs(Head:GetDescendants())

			for _, v in v1937, v1938, v1939 do
				if v:IsA("SpecialMesh") or v:IsA("MeshPart") then
					local MeshId = v:IsA("SpecialMesh") and v.MeshId or v.MeshId

					if MeshId and v1933(MeshId) then
						v:Destroy()
						u1935 = true
					end
				end
			end
		end

		local v1943, v1944, v1945 = ipairs(p171:GetChildren())
		local v1946 = geniter(v1943, v1944, v1945)
		local exitTo
		local Handle, v1949

		for _, v in v1943, v1944, v1945 do
			if v:IsA("Accessory") then
				Handle = v:FindFirstChild("Handle")

				if Handle then
					v1949 = v
					exitTo = 1

					break
				end
			end
		end

		if exitTo == 1 then
			local exitTo2

			repeat
				local SpecialMesh = Handle:FindFirstChildOfClass("SpecialMesh")

				if SpecialMesh and v1933(SpecialMesh.MeshId) then
					v1949:Destroy()
					u1935 = true
				end

				exitTo2 = nil

				while true do
					local v1954 = table.pack(v1946())

					if not v1954[1] then
						break
					end

					v1949 = v1954[3]

					if v1949:IsA("Accessory") then
						Handle = v1949:FindFirstChild("Handle")

						if Handle then
							exitTo2 = 1

							break
						end
					end
				end
			until exitTo2 ~= 1

			local Humanoid = p171:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

			if Humanoid then
				pcall(function()
					local appliedDescription = Humanoid:GetAppliedDescription()

					if appliedDescription then
						if v1933(appliedDescription.Head) then
							if Head then
								for _, descendant in ipairs(Head:GetDescendants()) do
									if descendant:IsA("BasePart") or descendant:IsA("MeshPart") or descendant:IsA("SpecialMesh") then
										pcall(function()
											descendant.Transparency = 1
										end)
									end
								end

								Head.Transparency = 1
							end

							u1935 = true
						end
					end
				end)
			end

			if u1935 then
				v1930(p172)
			end
		else
			local Humanoid = p171:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

			if Humanoid then
				pcall(function()
					local appliedDescription = Humanoid:GetAppliedDescription()

					if appliedDescription then
						if v1933(appliedDescription.Head) then
							if Head then
								for _, descendant in ipairs(Head:GetDescendants()) do
									if descendant:IsA("BasePart") or descendant:IsA("MeshPart") or descendant:IsA("SpecialMesh") then
										pcall(function()
											descendant.Transparency = 1
										end)
									end
								end

								Head.Transparency = 1
							end

							u1935 = true
						end
					end
				end)
			end

			if u1935 then
				v1930(p172)

				return
			end
		end
	end
	local function v1963(p173, p174)
		local v1964 = false
		local playingAnimationTracks = p173:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()

		for _, playingAnimationTrack in ipairs(playingAnimationTracks) do
			if playingAnimationTrack.Animation and v1932(playingAnimationTrack.Animation.AnimationId) then
				playingAnimationTrack:Stop(0)
				pcall(function()
					playingAnimationTrack:Destroy()
				end)
				v1964 = true
			end
		end

		if v1964 then
			v1930(p174)
		end
	end
	local function v1968()
		for _, v in ipairs(t70) do
			v:Disconnect()
		end

		table.clear(t70)
		table.clear(t71)

		local function v1971(player)
			if player == LocalPlayer3 then
				return
			end

			local function v1972(character)
				task.wait(0.5)
				v1934(character, player)

				local Humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

				if Humanoid then
					v1963(Humanoid, player)

					local connection2 = Humanoid.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(p175)
						if p175.Animation and v1932(p175.Animation.AnimationId) then
							p175:Stop(0)
							pcall(function()
								p175:Destroy()
							end)
							v1930(player)
						end
					end)

					table.insert(t70, connection2)
				end

				local connection2 = character.DescendantAdded:Connect(function()
					task.wait(0.1)
					v1934(character, player)
				end)

				table.insert(t70, connection2)
			end

			if player.Character then
				v1972(player.Character)
			end

			table.insert(t70, player.CharacterAdded:Connect(v1972))
		end

		for _, player in ipairs(Players4:GetPlayers()) do
			v1971(player)
		end

		table.insert(t70, Players4.PlayerAdded:Connect(v1971))
		table.insert(t70, Players4.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
			t71[player.Name] = nil
		end))

		local connection2 = RunService2.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
			if not u1922 then
				return
			end

			for _, player in ipairs(Players4:GetPlayers()) do
				if player ~= LocalPlayer3 and player.Character then
					local Humanoid = player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

					if Humanoid then
						v1963(Humanoid, player)
					end
				end
			end
		end)

		table.insert(t70, connection2)
	end
	local function v1982()
		for _, v in ipairs(t70) do
			v:Disconnect()
		end

		table.clear(t70)
		table.clear(t71)
	end

	v48:AddToggle({
		Name = "Anti Flashbang / Skybox",
		Description = "wein",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(p176)
			u1922 = p176

			if p176 then
				v1968()
			else
				v1982()
			end
		end
	})
end

	local function v1989(p177)
		if p177 then
			if p177:IsDescendantOf(game) then
				local t72 = {}

					if child:IsA("Tool") then
						table.insert(t72, child)

					for i = 16, #t72 do

							t72[i]:Destroy()
						end)
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

				end
			end
		end
	end

	}
	local s7 = "Praça"

	local function v2006(p179)
		local Character = LocalPlayer3.Character

		if Character and Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
			local HumanoidRootPart = Character.HumanoidRootPart

	end
